    <script>self["MonacoEnvironment"] = (function (paths) {
          return {
            globalAPI: false,
            getWorkerUrl : function (moduleId, label) {
              var result =  paths[label];
              if (/^((http:)|(https:)|(file:)|(\/\/))/.test(result)) {
                var currentUrl = String(window.location);
                var currentOrigin = currentUrl.substr(0, currentUrl.length - window.location.hash.length - window.location.search.length - window.location.pathname.length);
                if (result.substring(0, currentOrigin.length) !== currentOrigin) {
                  var js = '/*' + label + '*/importScripts("' + result + '");';
                  var blob = new Blob([js], { type: 'application/javascript' });
                  return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                }
              }
              return result;
            }
          };

    <!-- Runtime config — must execute before the Vite module scripts read
         window.__APP_CONFIG__. `defer` keeps that order (deferred classic
         scripts and module scripts run in document order after parsing) while
         unblocking the HTML parser, so asset preloads start immediately
         instead of stalling ~160ms (p75) on this API round trip.
         Back on the .js path: Render's extension-keyed edge cache rule twice
         pinned a wrong body under this URL despite `no-store` (2026-08-20 and
         2026-08-24); Render has since removed that cache rule for us, so the
         suffix is safe again. If poisoning recurs: bump the `?v=` cache key,
         or switch the src to the extension-less /api/frontend-config route
         (kept serving as an escape hatch). -->
    <script src="./7000 _ Base44_files/frontend-config.js.download" defer=""></script>

    <!-- Platform favicon: a real multi-size ICO (16/32/48) of the current sun
         mark, served from public/. This is the ONLY rel="icon" link, and it
         must stay that way — App.js rewrites the first match for backoffice.
         Crawlers and link scrapers read this tag (and probe /favicon.ico
         directly) with no JS, so the brand has to be correct statically. -->
    <link rel="icon" href="https://app.base44.com/favicon.ico" sizes="any">
    <script>
      // Pre-paint theme stamp for the demo Appearance (demo-appearance-themes)
      // cohort: on a hard load the theme is applied by React (DemoThemeApplier)
      // only after auth resolves, so the document boots light — a white flash,
      // then light route skeletons (the FOUC this removes). DemoThemeApplier
      // persists 'demo-display-theme-applied' only while the feature is active,
      // so every other cohort (flag-off, native, backoffice, runtime apps)
      // never sets it and this stays a no-op. React stays the source of truth
      // after mount: DemoThemeApplier
      // re-applies or clears these exact attributes. Keys must stay byte-identical
      // with components/settings/useDemoTheme.ts — BOOT_STAMP_CONTRACT there is
      // asserted against this file by useDemoTheme.spec.ts, so update both.
      //
      // Kill-switch caveat: the marker is written while the feature is active and
      // only cleared once /me resolves. On the FIRST load after a user loses the
      // flag — or after the kill switch is flipped mid-incident — this still
      // stamps dark, then React settles to light: one dark→light flash, which
      // self-heals from the next load on. Flipping the switch does not repaint
      // already-open tabs pre-paint; it collapses them on their next load.
      try {
        if (localStorage.getItem('demo-display-theme-applied') === '1') {
          var demoTheme = localStorage.getItem('demo-display-theme');
          var demoDark = demoTheme === 'dark' ||
            (demoTheme === 'system' && window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
          if (demoDark) {
            var demoRoot = document.documentElement;
            demoRoot.setAttribute('data-ds', 'base44');
            demoRoot.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
            demoRoot.classList.add('dark');
            // Kill the pre-CSS white document flash; the app paints over it.
            demoRoot.style.backgroundColor = '#09090b';
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    </script>

base44-markdown relative"><p>Here's how to get your app onto GitHub and run it locally:</p>
<p><strong>1. Connect to GitHub (in Base44)</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Click <strong>Dashboard</strong> in your app editor</li>
<li>Click the <strong>GitHub</strong> icon (top-right) → <strong>Connect to GitHub</strong> → <strong>Connect GitHub</strong> → <strong>Authorize Base44 Builder</strong></li>
<li>Pick your GitHub account/org and which repos to allow → <strong>Install</strong></li>
<li>Create a new repository (choose account, name it, e.g. <code>7000-bus-receipt</code>) → <strong>Create Repository</strong></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>2. Run it locally</strong></p>
<pre><code class="language-bash">git clone &lt;your-repo-url&gt;
cd &lt;repo-name&gt;
npm install
npm install -g base44@latest
base44 login
base44 link
base44 dev
</code></pre>
<p><strong>3. Sync changes back</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Push/merge changes to the <code>main</code> branch — they appear in your Base44 app automatically (only <code>main</code> is supported, not <code>master</code>)</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Note:</strong> GitHub 2-way sync requires a <strong>Builder plan or higher</strong>, and only the app owner can do the initial connection. Once it's connected and you've cloned the repo, you'll have the full code to run and edit locally. Want me to explain any of these steps in more detail?</p></div><div dir="ltr"></div></div></div></div><div class="flex items-center w-full gap-space-2 transition-opacity justify-start"><span base44-markdown relative"><p>Your screen already closely matches the iOS Messages look from your screenshot. To push it closer to the real iOS Messages app, here are the authentic touches I'd add:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>iOS status bar</strong> at the very top — live clock on the left, cellular/WiFi/battery glyphs on the right, sitting above the header (currently missing).</li>
<li><strong>"Delivered" receipts</strong> — small gray "Delivered" text under each outgoing green bubble, right-aligned (iOS shows this under sent SMS).</li>
<li><strong>Bubble grouping</strong> — consecutive bubbles from the same sender get tighter spacing and shared corners (only the first/last in a group gets the "tail" corner), exactly how iOS chains messages.</li>
<li><strong>Bubble tails</strong> — a subtle notch on the last bubble of each group (the small pointy corner iOS uses), rather than every bubble having the same rounded corner.</li>
<li><strong>Input bar polish</strong> — match iOS's exact pill shape, the "+" sits just left of the field, and the mic/send button toggles based on text (already done).</li>
</ol>
<p>The colors (black bg, <code>#262629</code> incoming, <code>#34C759</code> green SMS, <code>#0A84FF</code> blue links) and the header/avatar layout are already accurate, so these are refinements on top of what's there.</p>
<p>Want me to go ahead and build these refinements? If so, which matter most to you — all five, or just the status bar and delivered receipts?</p></div><div dir="ltr"></div></div></div></div><div class="flex items-center w-full gap-space-2
